# Annex B — Consolidated food inventory

One food can appear under more than one substance. PM appearances count distinct primary mechanisms. Sections 1.1 and 3.1 of the same atom are one relationship. RSP values are not added together. PM-specific target dose / effective intake: **Not established** for every row.

Plant origin follows the food page tag `Vegan`. Pages without that tag are recorded as animal or mixed. That is a page classification, not a new biological claim.

## Comparison groups

Each group is one substance, one unit and one exact basis. The filter column marks the provisional reporting subset (RSP at least 10, at most five). Foods below that line remain in the distribution.
